- **triage-captain**: Read-only issue triager. Reads a repo's open issues with gh, classifies each one, confirms duplicates, and returns a triage plan with labels and reply drafts. ([vivekbhange-hub/maintainer-skill](https://github.com/vivekbhange-hub/maintainer-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-captain.md))
- **triage-classifier**: Triage Classifier of the Software Factory. Use it first on every new request the Team Lead receives (except a plain status question) to classify the work - type, size, the lifecycle path, which conditional reviewers are needed, and whether it is clear enough to start - so small work exits early and the reviewer selection is deterministic. Read-only and fast; it never plans, designs or writes code. ([achisolomon/open-software-factory](https://github.com/achisolomon/open-software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/triage-classifier.md) · ★8)
- **triage-classifier**: Vendor reply parser + state-machine updater. Given pasted text from a platform (HackerOne / Bugcrowd / Intigriti / HITCON ZD / TWCERT / direct email), classify the triage outcome (N/A / Informative / Duplicate / Triaged / Resolved / Need-More-Info / Bounty-Awarded), extract bounty/CVSS/CVE if mentioned, propose Submission frontmatter patch, propose Kanban move, and surface KB-promotion candidates. Returns structured proposals — operator confirms before mutation. Use when user pastes a vendor reply or says "vendor replied", "triage result", "got N/A", "Accepted", "Duplicate", "bounty received"… ([guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework](https://github.com/guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-classifier.md) · ★2)

- **triage-classifier**: Read one file from Clippings/ and propose a triage classification (keep/delete + destination subdirectory). Read-only — proposes but does not act. ([YurainSoon/llm-wiki-vault](https://github.com/YurainSoon/llm-wiki-vault/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-classifier.md) · ★1)
- **triage-classifier**: Confirms or adjusts the computed review tier for a change using judgment about blast radius. Returns a structured review plan. Lit, filtre, et ne renvoie que les extraits pertinents avec leurs chemins et numéros de ligne. - **triage-labels**: The five-state vocabulary this repo uses for issue triage. Every issue carries exactly one state label.
